{"parentUuid":"b44986a9-04cd-49eb-9ecc-6e10a1e55ed6","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"324c54cc-6671-466e-8317-d697a935d548","timestamp":"2026-05-06T05:56:40.988Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"324c54cc-6671-466e-8317-d697a935d548","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Codified as `sync-039` confirmed-aligned in `sync-state.md`. re_verify_when: sessions/ count exceeds 200 OR grep performance painful.\n\n### Q-D2 — Obsidian Wiki Scaling at 200+ Entities\n\n- **fire_when**: Phase 1 entry OR when wiki/ count > 100 entities (whichever first)\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-30 (S24 entry AskUserQuestion bundle Q3)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-30 (same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster D\n- **question**: Does Obsidian raw/wiki Karpathy pattern scale at 200+ entities?\n- **options**: A: Yes, current pattern proven; just need disciplined index updates / B: Add tiered wiki/ (tier1-canonical/tier2-derived/tier3-thesis) / C: Replace with KG (DuckDB+pgvector? RDF triplestore?) / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Yes, current pattern proven; just need disciplined index updates. spec-to-wiki skill enforces wikilink convention; periodic _index.md re-render via hook. KG migration only if Obsidian text-search becomes inadequate for cross-entity thesis synthesis. Codified as `sync-040` confirmed-aligned in `sync-state.md`. re_verify_when: wiki/ entity count exceeds 200 AND _index.md staleness > 1 week.\n\n### Q-D3 — Memory Tier Codification\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `memory-tiers.md` authoring (Track 7 amendment per REV-2) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster D\n- **question**: Codify Tier 1 (immutable always-loaded) / Tier 2 (just-in-time) / Tier 3 (explicit pull)?\n- **options**: A: Yes — add `memory-tiers.md` to constitution / B: Yes AND add hook to enforce / C: Already implicit in CLAUDE.md + autonomous-protocol — no new artifact / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Yes, add `memory-tiers.md` to constitution as formal artifact codifying always-loaded vs just-in-time vs explicit-pull tiers. To be drafted in `proposals/memory-tiers.md` during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E1 — Self-Detect-Drift Mechanism\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `self-application-bootstrap.md` authoring OR S8 Track 9 self-awareness implementation (whichever first) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: How agents auto-detect drift WITHOUT human prompt?\n- **options**: A: Per-task DA-rule (re-read spec every 5 steps) + checkpoint-vs-current diff hook / B: Mid-session `/session-verify` auto-fired by Stop hook every N tool calls / C: Fresh-context drift-auditor subagent dispatched mid-session at random/triggered intervals / D: All A+B+C / E: open answer\n- **answer**: D (Recommended) — All A+B+C combined; defense-in-depth matches stockforge multi-perspective principle. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Self-detect-drift mechanism during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E2 — Agent-Notes Promotion Frequency\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: How often does agent-notes → skill/hook/deterministic promotion run?\n- **options**: A: Phase-boundary only (manual review by promotion subagent, opus, fresh ctx) / B: Per-session-end Stop hook scans new agent-notes; dispatches subagent if ≥3 similar entries / C: Continuous — every new note triggers similarity check / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Phase-boundary only; manual review by promotion subagent. Cheapest; matches stockforge phase cadence. To be codified in `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § Promotion frequency during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E3 — Promotion Target Priority\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 (paired with E2) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: When agent-note becomes promotable, where does it land?\n- **options**: A: New skill in `.claude/skills/<name>/` / B: New hook script in `scripts/hooks/` / C: Update existing constitution file / D: All three valid; promotion subagent decides per pattern type — but priority MUST be hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST (cheapest first) / E: open answer\n- **answer**: D (Recommended; rephrased as \"Hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST\") — cheapest deterministic check first; skill encodes procedural discipline; charter amendment heaviest. To be codified in `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § Promotion target priority during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E4 — Auto-Detected-Drift Recovery Flow\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 (`autonomous-protocol.md` recovery section) OR S8 Track 9 (paired with E1) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 3, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: If drift auto-detected mid-session, recovery flow?\n- **options**: A: HALT immediately; emit ALERT; require human resume / B: HALT only if charter-tier; SOFT-WARN otherwise / C: Open Q&A bundle with detected drift + 3 remediation options; let human pick async / D: Auto-revert to last clean checkpoint + emit notification / E: open answer\n- **answer**: C (Recommended) — Open Q&A bundle with detected drift + 3 remediation options; let human pick async. Matches UP-06 NO-Silent-Default + mobile-remote use case. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Drift recovery flow during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-2.1 — Skill-Tool Autonomous Mode Policy\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `autonomous-protocol.md` authoring → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2, same turn)\n- **source**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-04-29-003-up05-askuser-permissions-rawsessions.md` cluster Q2\n- **question**: For autonomous run, how to handle Skill tool prompts (Anthropic-designed always-prompt)?\n- **options**: A: Avoid Skill calls in autonomous mode (replace with inline procedures + agent dispatch) / B: Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills / C: Batch Skill calls at session-start under explicit user approval (one-time per session) / D: Accept friction — pause on Skill prompt; not deal-breaker / E: open answer\n- **answer**: B (Recommended) — Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills (replace with inline procedures or subagent dispatch). Matches existing agent-notes UP-05 rule. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Skill-tool policy in autonomous mode during S16 IMPL.\n\n---\n\n## Closed (after answer)\n\n(none yet — append entries here as questions get fired + answered)\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Note for SessionStart Hook (Track 5, S3)\n\nHook script `scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh` (Track 5 deliverable) MUST include:\n\n```bash\n# Check queued-grill triggers\nQUEUED_GRILL_FILE=\"agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\"\nACTIVE_PHASE=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:/ {print $2}' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Active plan\\*\\*/' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\n\n# For each ### Q-* section, parse fire_when and check match\n# If match → emit notification: \"queued-grill ready: <Q-id>; consider firing AskUserQuestion in this session\"\n# Do NOT auto-fire — surface to agent who decides timing\n```\n\nThis keeps the queue auditable + actionable without LLM-Guardian continuous polling.\n","numLines":190,"startLine":1,"totalLines":190}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1c6a88c1-89f6-4f62-b7cb-34bb76664aea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"eb05685c-e330-4ebd-bbec-5c29e0b6c27b","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"7adedc1c-d68a-4355-a41f-9fed54a0628f","timestamp":"2026-05-06T05:56:52.546Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

The LLM SHALL NOT execute that next_action, perform Agent.dispatch, run Bash final-checks, or edit any other file in the same turn after checkpoint write. One short summary message to user is permitted; no additional tool calls.\"\n- [ ] T4.2: Companion 3 hooks in `scripts/hooks/`:\n  - [ ] T4.2a: `checkpoint-write-marker.sh` — PostToolUse hook gates on Edit/Write to `checkpoints/latest.md`; emits marker file `agent-workspace/memory/.checkpoint-written-<session_id>` with timestamp.\n  - [ ] T4.2b: `checkpoint-write-end-turn-watchdog.sh` — PostToolUse hook fires after T4.2a's marker exists; if subsequent tool call within same session is Edit/Write/Agent/Bash (non-trivial), emit drift-signal severity HIGH \"L-S49b-4 carve-out violated\".\n  - [ ] T4.2c: `checkpoint-marker-cleanup-resume.sh` — SessionStart hook on resume/clear chain; clears stale `.checkpoint-written-*` markers from prior sessions; reads checkpoint's `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` field and surfaces any `status=PENDING` rows older than 2hr as `<system-reminder>` to the LLM.\n- [ ] T4.3: Firing-tests for all 3 hooks (firing-tests directory pattern from T2.5).\n- [ ] T4.4: **AskUserQuestion gate** — explicit ratify of charter Rule N+1. Multi-batch question if needed: (Q1) Ratify exact wording? (Q2) Ratify hook scaffold? (Q3) Approve ADR D-NNN authoring with sequence number?\n- [ ] T4.5: Post-ratify: ADR D-NNN authored at `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-checkpoint-boundary-charter-rule.md`; mv proposal → applied.\n\n**Output artifact**: charter amendment file + 3 hooks + 3 firing-tests + ADR.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**:\n- AC-T4.1: User explicit ratification via AskUserQuestion (letter pick, no default-acceptance per Q-B2 closed answer).\n- AC-T4.2: All 3 firing-tests exit 0.","numLines":200,"startLine":1,"totalLines":444}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2a1e6ea1-f926-4121-a699-32d97d9d76f2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

-f \"$f\" ] && continue\n28\t  if grep -qiE '(approximately|roughly|around|circa)\\s+[0-9]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n29\t    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n30\t    printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-math anti-pattern) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n31\t  fi\n32\t  # I-S1: confidence claim without n_samples / hit_rate / lookback metadata.\n33\t  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n34\t    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n35\t      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n36\t      printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (confidence-without-calibration) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n37\t    fi\n38\t  fi\n39\t  # I-S2: numeric values without source: / as_of: in same file.\n40\t  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n41\t    if ! grep -qE '(source:|as[_ ]of:)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n42\t      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n43\t      printf '[%s] I-S2-VIOLATION (numeric-no-citation) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n44\t    fi\n45\t  fi\n46\t  # I-S10: thesis without bear case (only for thesis-log/).\n47\t  case \"$f\" in\n48\t    */thesis-log/*)\n49\t      if ! grep -qiE '(bear[_ ]case|risks?|downside)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n50\t        VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n51\t        printf '[%s] I-S10-VIOLATION (thesis-no-bear-case) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n52\t      fi\n53\t      ;;\n54\t  esac\n55\tdone <<< \"$CHANGED_FILES\"\n56\t\n57\tif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n58\t  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] taskcompleted-audit: $VIOLATIONS violation(s) found across recent files\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n59\tfi\n60\t\n61\texit 0\n62\t"}]},"uuid":"caa8981f-1223-43a3-be87-fa49d599dab1","timestamp":"2026-05-06T05:57:49.376Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\taskcompleted-audit.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# taskcompleted-audit.sh — runs after task/subagent completion to grep changed files\n# for I-S1 (LLM math) + I-S2 (missing citations) violations.\n# Wired as Stop hook (poor man's TaskCompleted; Claude Code v2.x has no TaskCompleted event).\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B deliverable.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.taskcompleted-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\n# Gather files changed in last hour (heuristic for \"this turn's changes\").\n# Only audit thesis-log + specs + agent-workspace/memory data files.\nCHANGED_FILES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n                       \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" \\\n                       \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\" \\\n                  -type f -name '*.md' -mmin -60 2>/dev/null | head -20)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CHANGED_FILES\" ] && exit 0\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\n# I-S1: LLM math anti-pattern — \"approximately N\", \"around N\", \"roughly N\", \"N% confidence\" without metadata.\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] || [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '(approximately|roughly|around|circa)\\s+[0-9]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-math anti-pattern) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\n  # I-S1: confidence claim without n_samples / hit_rate / lookback metadata.\n  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n      printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (confidence-without-calibration) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    fi\n  fi\n  # I-S2: numeric values without source: / as_of: in same file.\n  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! Non-blocking (exit 0 always).\n# Output: warnings to .session-hooks.log + per-violation note to NOTIF_DIR if any found.\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + node-eval + POSIX only (per L-S11-1).\n# L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nLOOP_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/loop\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Skip if directory missing or empty (no framings yet emitted = no violation possible).\nif [ ! -d \"$LOOP_DIR\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n\nif [ -d \"$LOOP_DIR\" ]; then\n  while IFS= read -r -d '' file; do\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$file\")\"\n    # Frontmatter is the first --- ... --- block; grep for the field within first 30 lines.\n    head_block=\"$(head -n 30 \"$file\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    metric_line=\"\"\n    metric_line=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$head_block\" | grep -E '^metric_function:' | head -n 1 || true)\"\n    # Violation if line absent OR value is empty / null / \"not-yet-authored\" / placeholder.\n    is_violation=0\n    if [ -z \"$metric_line\" ]; then\n      is_violation=1\n    else\n      val=\"$(printf '%s' \"$metric_line\" | sed 's/^metric_function:[[:space:]]*//' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*#.*$//' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//' | tr -d '\"' | tr -d \"'\")\"\n      case \"$val\" in\n        \"\"|null|none|tbd|TBD|\"not-yet-authored\"|\"not yet authored\")\n          is_violation=1\n          ;;\n      esac\n    fi\n    if [ \"$is_violation\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n      VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${bn}: missing or placeholder metric_function field\"$'\\n'\n    fi\n  done < <(find \"$LOOP_DIR\" -name '*-experiment-frame.md' -type f -print0 2>/dev/null)\nfi\n\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] learning-loop-metric-check WARN %d framing(s) without metric_function:\\n%s' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"$VIOLATIONS\" \"$VIOLATION_LIST\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  if [ -d \"$NOTIF_DIR\" ]; then\n    TS_FILE=\"$(date -u +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n    NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/$TS_FILE-loop-metric-check-warn.md\"\n    {\n      printf '# Learning-Loop Metric-Function Check — Warnings\\n\\n'\n      printf 'Per L-S12-1 (agent-notes 2026-04-29): every Karpathy framing artifact MUST cite a deterministic metric function.\\n\\n'\n      printf '%d framing artifact(s) lack a usable `metric_function:` field:\\n\\n' \"$VIOLATIONS\"\n      printf '%s' \"$VIOLATION_LIST\"\n      printf '\\nFix: edit each frame to add `metric_function: <path-or-inline-name>` in frontmatter; reference the metric script at `scripts/hooks/metric-failure-mode-rate.sh` or sibling.\\n'\n    } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nelse\n  printf '[%s] learning-loop-metric-check OK (0 violations)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":74,"startLine":1,"totalLines":74}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1564e118-507a-4e19-aa6c-8efbc868f226","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\n19\tset -euo pipefail\n20\t\n21\tSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n22\tHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../proposal-bundle-advisor.sh\"\n23\t[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n24\t\n25\tTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\n26\ttrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n27\t\n28\tPROP_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/proposals\"\n29\tMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n30\tLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n31\t\n32\tclean_state() {\n33\t  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n34\t  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\"\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\t# --- TC1: no PROP_DIR → silent exit 0 ---\n38\tclean_state\n39\tRC=0\n40\tCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\n41\tif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n42\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n43\t  exit 1\n44\tfi\n45\tif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"proposal-bundle-advisor\" \"$LOG\"; then\n46\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should be empty when no PROP_DIR\"\n47\t  cat \"$LOG\"\n48\t  exit 1\n49\tfi\n50\techo \"PASS TC1: no PROP_DIR → silent exit 0\"\n51\t\n52\t# --- TC2: empty PROP_DIR → log pending=0 ---\n53\tclean_state\n54\tmkdir -p \"$PROP_DIR\"\n55\tRC=0\n56\tCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\n57\tif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n58\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n59\t  exit 1\n60\tfi\n61\tif ! poll_log \"rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0\" 6; then\n62\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should record rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0 within 6s\"\n63\t  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n64\t  exit 1\n65\tfi\n66\techo \"PASS TC1: empty dirs → rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0\"\n67\t\n68\t# --- TC2: fresh event (mtime now) + ROTATE_DAYS=7 → no rotate ---\n69\tclean_state\n70\techo '{\"event\":\"foo\"}' > \"$EVENTS_DIR/fresh.ndjson\"\n71\tRC=0\n72\tCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\n73\tif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n74\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n75\t  exit 1\n76\tfi\n77\tif ! poll_log \"rotated=0 purged=0\" 6; then\n78\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should record rotated=0 purged=0 within 6s\"\n79\t  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n80\t  exit 1\n81\tfi\n82\tsleep 1\n83\tif [ ! -f \"$EVENTS_DIR/fresh.ndjson\" ]; then\n84\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: fresh event should remain in events/\"\n85\t  exit 1\n86\tfi\n87\techo \"PASS TC2: fresh event → no rotate\"\n88\t\n89\t# --- TC3: stale event (mtime 8d ago) → rotated to archive/ ---\n90\tclean_state\n91\techo '{\"event\":\"old\"}' > \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\"\n92\ttouch -d \"8 days ago\" \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\" 2>/dev/null || touch -t \"$(date -v-8d +%Y%m%d%H%M)\" \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\"\n93\tRC=0\n94\tCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\n95\tif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n96\t  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n97\t  exit 1\n98\tfi\n99\tif ! Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../learning-queue-sweeper.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEVENTS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/events\"\nARCHIVE_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/archive\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR\" \"$MEM_DIR\"\n}\n\n# Poll log for pattern (subshell is backgrounded + disowned; needs grace period).\n# Returns 0 when pattern found within timeout, 1 otherwise.\npoll_log() {\n  local pattern=\"$1\"\n  local timeout=\"${2:-6}\"\n  local count=0\n  while [ \"$count\" -lt \"$timeout\" ]; do\n    if [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"$pattern\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      return 0\n    fi\n    sleep 1\n    count=$(( count + 1 ))\n  done\n  return 1\n}\n\n# --- TC1: empty EVENTS_DIR + ARCHIVE_DIR → rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0 ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should record rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: empty dirs → rotated=0 purged=0 size_warn=0\"\n\n# --- TC2: fresh event (mtime now) + ROTATE_DAYS=7 → no rotate ---\nclean_state\necho '{\"event\":\"foo\"}' > \"$EVENTS_DIR/fresh.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"rotated=0 purged=0\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should record rotated=0 purged=0 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nsleep 1\nif [ ! -f \"$EVENTS_DIR/fresh.ndjson\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: fresh event should remain in events/\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: fresh event → no rotate\"\n\n# --- TC3: stale event (mtime 8d ago) → rotated to archive/ ---\nclean_state\necho '{\"event\":\"old\"}' > \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\"\ntouch -d \"8 days ago\" \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\" 2>/dev/null || touch -t \"$(date -v-8d +%Y%m%d%H%M)\" \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"rotated=1\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should record rotated=1 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nsleep 1\nif [ -f \"$EVENTS_DIR/stale.ndjson\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: stale event should be moved out of events/\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/stale.ndjson\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: stale event should now be in archive/\"\n  ls -la \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: stale event (8d) → rotated to archive/\"\n\n# --- TC4: old archive (mtime 31d ago) + PURGE_DAYS=30 → purged ---\nclean_state\necho '{\"event\":\"ancient\"}' > \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/ancient.ndjson\"\ntouch -d \"31 days ago\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/ancient.ndjson\" 2>/dev/null || touch -t \"$(date -v-31d +%Y%m%d%H%M)\" \"$ARCHIVE_DIR/ancient.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../taskcompleted-audit.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nTHESIS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\nDECISION_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\nSPECS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nAUDIT_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.taskcompleted-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0; no log entries ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: audit log should not exist when no recent files\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook log should not contain taskcompleted-audit entry\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean thesis (bear_case + source + as_of) → no violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/clean.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: VIC\nsource: cafef.vn/foo\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Hold thesis on VIC\nLong position considered.\nbear_case: rate hike sensitivity noted.\ndownside: macro shock could compress multiples.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: audit log should not exist for clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: hook log should not flag clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean thesis → no violation\"\n\n# --- TC3: decision with LLM-math \"approximately 8\" → I-S1 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-test.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nsource: cafef.vn/baz\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Decision: approximately 8 percent allocation\nPosition size around 8 of book per L-S12-1 doctrine.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: audit log should exist after I-S1 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-math anti-pattern)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: audit log should record I-S1 LLM-math anti-pattern\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: decision with 'approximately 8' → I-S1 LLM-math violation logged\"\n\n# --- TC4: thesis-log/ without bear_case → I-S10 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/missing-bear.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: HPG\nsource: cafef.vn/hpg\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Long thesis on HPG\nStrong fundamentals; favorable supply/demand dynamics.\nSteel margins expanding.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: audit log should exist after I-S10 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S10-VIOLATION (thesis-no-bear-case)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: audit log should record I-S10 thesis-no-bear-case\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: thesis-log without bear_case → I-S10 violation logged\"\n\n# --- TC5: decision with \"25%\" no source/as_of → I-S2 numeric-no-citation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-bare.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: PROPOSAL\n---\n\n# Decision: portfolio rebalance\nTarget allocation: 25% growth, 35% value, balance defensive.\nExpected drawdown: 15% peak-to-trough.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: audit log should exist after I-S2 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S2-VIOLATION (numeric-no-citation)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../taskcompleted-audit.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nTHESIS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\nDECISION_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\nSPECS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nAUDIT_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.taskcompleted-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0; no log entries ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: audit log should not exist when no recent files\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook log should not contain taskcompleted-audit entry\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean thesis (bear_case + source + as_of) → no violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/clean.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: VIC\nsource: cafef.vn/foo\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Hold thesis on VIC\nLong position considered.\nbear_case: rate hike sensitivity noted.\ndownside: macro shock could compress multiples.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: audit log should not exist for clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: hook log should not flag clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean thesis → no violation\"\n\n# --- TC3: decision with LLM-math \"approximately 8\" → I-S1 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-test.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nsource: cafef.vn/baz\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Decision: approximately 8 percent allocation\nPosition size around 8 of book per L-S12-1 doctrine.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../taskcompleted-audit.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nTHESIS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\nDECISION_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\nSPECS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nAUDIT_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.taskcompleted-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0; no log entries ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: audit log should not exist when no recent files\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook log should not contain taskcompleted-audit entry\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean thesis (bear_case + source + as_of) → no violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/clean.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: VIC\nsource: cafef.vn/foo\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Hold thesis on VIC\nLong position considered.\nbear_case: rate hike sensitivity noted.\ndownside: macro shock could compress multiples.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: audit log should not exist for clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: hook log should not flag clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean thesis → no violation\"\n\n# --- TC3: decision with LLM-math \"approximately 8\" → I-S1 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-test.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nsource: cafef.vn/baz\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Decision: approximately 8 percent allocation\nPosition size around 8 of book per L-S12-1 doctrine.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: audit log should exist after I-S1 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-math anti-pattern)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: audit log should record I-S1 LLM-math anti-pattern\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: decision with 'approximately 8' → I-S1 LLM-math violation logged\"\n\n# --- TC4: thesis-log/ without bear_case → I-S10 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/missing-bear.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: HPG\nsource: cafef.vn/hpg\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Long thesis on HPG\nStrong fundamentals; favorable supply/demand dynamics.\nSteel margins expanding.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: audit log should exist after I-S10 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S10-VIOLATION (thesis-no-bear-case)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: audit log should record I-S10 thesis-no-bear-case\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: thesis-log without bear_case → I-S10 violation logged\"\n\n# --- TC5: decision with \"25%\" no source/as_of → I-S2 numeric-no-citation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-bare.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: PROPOSAL\n---\n\n# Decision: portfolio rebalance\nTarget allocation: 25% growth, 35% value, balance defensive.\nExpected drawdown: 15% peak-to-trough.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: audit log should exist after I-S2 violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"I-S2-VIOLATION (numeric-no-citation)\" \"$AUDIT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: audit log should record I-S2 numeric-no-citation\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: decision with 25% no citation → I-S2 numeric-no-citation logged\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (5/5) ===\"\necho \"taskcompleted-audit.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":34,"oldLines":8,"newStart":34,"newLines":16,"lines":[" HOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\""," "," clean_state() {","+  # Pre-create all 3 audit dirs to mimic production layout. S78).\n#\n# Hook purpose (Stop hook; Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B deliverable):\n#   - Find recent .md files (mmin -60) in: thesis-log/, specs/, decisions/\n#   - Grep each for I-S1 (LLM-math anti-pattern), I-S1 (confidence-without-calibration),\n#     I-S2 (numeric-no-citation), I-S10 (thesis-no-bear-case for thesis-log/ only).\n#   - Per-violation line appended to .taskcompleted-audit.log.\n#   - If any violations: count summary appended to .session-hooks.log.\n#   - Always exit 0 (non-blocking).\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no audit dirs (no thesis-log/specs/decisions) → silent exit 0; no log entries\n#   TC2 — clean thesis (bear_case + source + as_of + no LLM-math) → no violation logged\n#   TC3 — decision with \"approximately 8 something\" → I-S1 LLM-math violation logged\n#   TC4 — thesis-log/ entry without bear_case keywords → I-S10 violation logged\n#   TC5 — decision with \"25% return\" no source/as_of → I-S2 numeric-no-citation violation logged\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../taskcompleted-audit.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nTHESIS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\nDECISION_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\nSPECS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nAUDIT_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.taskcompleted-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  # Pre-create all 3 audit dirs to mimic production layout. Without all 3 present,\n  # `find dir1 dir2 dir3` exits rc=1 (multi-path: missing arg → error code) which\n  # under pipefail+ERR-trap causes hook silent-exit before any audit work. This is\n  # a multi-path variant of the L-S68-2 family (S68/S75 single-path missing dir,\n  # S76 ls-glob no-match). Fixture matches production where specs/ + thesis-log/\n  # + decisions/ are all well-known dirs (even if empty). NOT a hook bug — production\n  # invariant. Backlog candidate for bash-hook-lint extension to detect multi-path\n  # find without per-arg [ -d ] guard.\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$THESIS_DIR\" \"$DECISION_DIR\" \"$SPECS_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0; no log entries ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: audit log should not exist when no recent files\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook log should not contain taskcompleted-audit entry\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no audit dirs → silent exit 0\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean thesis (bear_case + source + as_of) → no violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/clean.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: VIC\nsource: cafef.vn/foo\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Hold thesis on VIC\nLong position considered.\nbear_case: rate hike sensitivity noted.\ndownside: macro shock could compress multiples.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$AUDIT_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: audit log should not exist for clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: hook log should not flag clean thesis\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean thesis → no violation\"\n\n# --- TC3: decision with LLM-math \"approximately 8\" → I-S1 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$DECISION_DIR\"\ncat > \"$DECISION_DIR/D-test.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nsource: cafef.vn/baz\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Decision: approximately 8 percent allocation\nPosition size around 8 of book per L-S12-1 doctrine.\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: audit log should record I-S1 LLM-math anti-pattern\"\n  cat \"$AUDIT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"taskcompleted-audit:.*violation\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: hook log should record violation count summary\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: decision with 'approximately 8' → I-S1 LLM-math violation logged\"\n\n# --- TC4: thesis-log/ without bear_case → I-S10 violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$THESIS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$THESIS_DIR/missing-bear.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nticker: HPG\nsource: cafef.vn/hpg\nas_of: 2026-01-01\n---\n\n# Long thesis on HPG\nStrong fundamentals; {"parentUuid":"6f4c4ec0-89bf-43a0-a5e4-e0d78bb7a63d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01FXLNXE8fPzgzfDhtBB199X","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WUYhv7DYqZRHKLdYngfKKP","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\learning-index-rebuild-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for learning-index-rebuild.sh (L-S49b-4 charter-coverage push; S78).\n#\n# Hook purpose (Stop hook; runs in BACKGROUND subshell + disown per D-005 § 5.5d.2):\n#   - Counts events across agent-workspace/learning-data/events/*.ndjson\n#   - Reads prior count from index/.last-index-build.json\n#   - SHOULD_REBUILD=1 if delta >= LEARNING_INDEX_N_EVENTS (default 100) OR LEARNING_INDEX_FORCE=1\n#   - Else: log SKIP delta=N total=M threshold=K\n#   - Rebuild path: writes manifest.json + recent-sample.ndjson + .last-index-build.json\n#     then logs OK delta=N total=M sample_lines=L. If delta >= threshold AND NOTIF_DIR exists:\n#     writes notification file to NOTIF_DIR.\n#\n# 5 test cases (uses poll loop because subshell is backgrounded with disown):\n#   TC1 — no EVENTS_DIR → SHOULD_REBUILD=0; log SKIP delta=0 total=0\n#   TC2 — fresh events under threshold (LEARNING_INDEX_N_EVENTS=100, 1 event) → SKIP\n#   TC3 — events delta >= threshold (override LEARNING_INDEX_N_EVENTS=3, 5 events) → REBUILD\n#         + manifest/sample/state written + notif written\n#   TC4 — LEARNING_INDEX_FORCE=1 with no events → REBUILD; log OK delta=0 total=0; no notif\n#   TC5 — prior state exists (events_at_build=10), current total=15, threshold=3 → REBUILD;\n#         new state events_at_build=15\n#\n# Pre-flight: node availability (hook depends on node JSON parse for prior state).\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../learning-index-rebuild.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\n# Pre-flight: node required for prior-state JSON parse.\nif ! command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"SKIP: node unavailable; hook depends on node for prior-state JSON parse\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEVENTS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/events\"\nINDEX_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/index\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nSTATE_FILE=\"$INDEX_DIR/.last-index-build.json\"\nMANIFEST=\"$INDEX_DIR/manifest.json\"\nSAMPLE=\"$INDEX_DIR/recent-sample.ndjson\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\n# Poll log for pattern (subshell is backgrounded + disowned; needs grace period).\npoll_log() {\n  local pattern=\"$1\"\n  local timeout=\"${2:-6}\"\n  local count=0\n  while [ \"$count\" -lt \"$timeout\" ]; do\n    if [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"$pattern\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      return 0\n    fi\n    sleep 1\n    count=$(( count + 1 ))\n  done\n  return 1\n}\n\n# Poll for a file's existence.\npoll_file() {\n  local path=\"$1\"\n  local timeout=\"${2:-6}\"\n  local count=0\n  while [ \"$count\" -lt \"$timeout\" ]; do\n    if [ -f \"$path\" ]; then\n      return 0\n    fi\n    sleep 1\n    count=$(( count + 1 ))\n  done\n  return 1\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no EVENTS_DIR → SHOULD_REBUILD=0; log SKIP ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"learning-index-rebuild SKIP delta=0 total=0\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should record SKIP delta=0 total=0 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nsleep 1\nif [ -f \"$MANIFEST\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: manifest should not be written on SKIP path\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no EVENTS_DIR → SKIP delta=0 total=0\"\n\n# --- TC2: 1 event + threshold=100 → SKIP ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\necho '{\"event\":\"foo\",\"ts\":1}' > \"$EVENTS_DIR/probe.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"learning-index-rebuild SKIP delta=1 total=1 threshold=100\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should record SKIP delta=1 total=1 threshold=100 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nsleep 1\nif [ -f \"$MANIFEST\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: manifest should not be written on SKIP path (under threshold)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: 1 event + threshold=100 → SKIP\"\n\n# --- TC3: 5 events + threshold=3 → REBUILD + manifest/sample/state + notif ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\n{\n  echo '{\"event\":\"a\",\"ts\":1}'\n  echo '{\"event\":\"b\",\"ts\":2}'\n  echo '{\"event\":\"c\",\"ts\":3}'\n} > \"$EVENTS_DIR/file1.ndjson\"\n{\n  echo '{\"event\":\"d\",\"ts\":4}'\n  echo '{\"event\":\"e\",\"ts\":5}'\n} > \"$EVENTS_DIR/file2.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nLEARNING_INDEX_N_EVENTS=3 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"learning-index-rebuild OK delta=5 total=5\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should record OK delta=5 total=5 within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_file \"$MANIFEST\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: manifest should be written within 6s\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_file \"$SAMPLE\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: sample should be written within 6s\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_file \"$STATE_FILE\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: state file should be written within 6s\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"events_at_build\":5' \"$STATE_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: state should record events_at_build=5\"\n  cat \"$STATE_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"total_events\":5' \"$MANIFEST\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: manifest should record total_events=5\"\n  cat \"$MANIFEST\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# notif file written when delta >= threshold AND NOTIF_DIR exists\nNOTIF_COUNT=0\nNOTIF_COUNT=$(find \"$NOTIF_DIR\" -name '*-learning-analysis-ready.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nif [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: notification file should be written when delta >= threshold\"\n  ls -la \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: 5 events + threshold=3 → REBUILD + manifest/sample/state + notif written\"\n\n# --- TC4: LEARNING_INDEX_FORCE=1 + no events → REBUILD; log OK delta=0 total=0; no notif ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\nRC=0\nLEARNING_INDEX_FORCE=1 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_log \"learning-index-rebuild OK delta=0 total=0\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log should record OK delta=0 total=0 (force rebuild) within 6s\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_file \"$MANIFEST\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: manifest should be written even on force+empty\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! poll_file \"$STATE_FILE\" 6; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: state file should be written even on force+empty\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"events_at_build\":0' \"$STATE_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: state should record events_at_build=0\"\n  cat \"$STATE_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nsleep 1\nNOTIF_COUNT=0\nNOTIF_COUNT=$(find \"$NOTIF_DIR\" -name '*-learning-analysis-ready.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nif [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -ne 0 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: notif should NOT be written when delta < threshold (delta=0, threshold=100)\"\n  ls -la \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: FORCE=1 + no events → REBUILD; OK delta=0 total=0; no notif\"\n\n# --- TC5: prior state events_at_build=10, current total=15, threshold=3 → REBUILD; new state=15 ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\" \"$INDEX_DIR\"\necho '{\"as_of\":\"2026-01-01\",\"events_at_build\":10,\"sample_lines\":10}' > \"$STATE_FILE\"\n{\n  for i in $(seq 1 15); do\n    echo \"{\\\"event\\\":\\\"e$i\\\",\\\"ts\\\":$i}\"\n  done\n} > \"$EVENTS_DIR/file.ndjson\"\nRC=0\nLEARNING_INDEX_N_EVENTS=3 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../learning-loop-metric-check.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOOP_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/loop\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no LOOP_DIR → silent exit 0; no log entry ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"learning-loop-metric-check\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should not contain entry when LOOP_DIR missing\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no LOOP_DIR → silent exit 0\"\n\n# --- TC2: empty LOOP_DIR → log OK (0 violations) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$LOOP_DIR\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"learning-loop-metric-check OK (0 violations)\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should record OK (0 violations) for empty LOOP_DIR\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: empty LOOP_DIR → log OK (0 violations)\"\n\n# --- TC3: framing with valid metric_function → log OK (0 violations) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$LOOP_DIR\"\ncat > \"$LOOP_DIR/2026-05-06-experiment-frame.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nexperiment_id: e1\nhypothesis: \"approach X reduces failure mode rate\"\nmetric_function: scripts/hooks/metric-failure-mode-rate.sh\nexpected_outcome: \"failure-mode-rate < baseline\"\n---\n\n# Experiment frame\nEOF\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! Non-blocking (exit 0 always).\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only (per L-S11-1).\n# L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nDOGFOOD_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nif [ ! -d \"$DOGFOOD_DIR\" ]; reference `.claude/agents/research-scanner.md` § Output for the canonical template.\\n'\n    } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nelse\n  printf '[%s] research-scanner-output-validator OK (0 violations)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":71,"startLine":1,"totalLines":71}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"36f7d41f-36f6-4759-8319-e03c9db2fd65","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"678a9533-aa12-492c-9d3e-ac00dbbe8988","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

then\n34\t  tail -n 200 \"$HOOK_LOG\" > \"$TARGET/session-hooks.tail\" 2>/dev/null || true\n35\tfi\n36\t\n37\t# Capture metadata.\n38\t{\n39\t  printf 'snapshot_id: %s\\n' \"$TS\"\n40\t  printf 'created_at: %s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n41\t  printf 'session_id: %s\\n' \"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n42\t  printf 'reason: PreCompact hook (context compaction imminent)\\n'\n43\t} > \"$TARGET/metadata.yaml\"\n44\t\n45\techo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] PreCompact: thesis state snapshotted to $TARGET\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n46\t\n47\t# Rotate: keep last 10 snapshots.\n48\tSNAPSHOT_COUNT=\"$(ls -1 \"$SNAPSHOT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\"\n49\tif [ \"${SNAPSHOT_COUNT:-0}\" -gt 10 ];
